# E05 R05 addendum A: full tables

*Numbers only. The summary is the "Addendum A" section of `../README.md`.*

**Status.** **Complete. Nothing was cut** (A1 at blocks 32, 24 and 16 for all six models; A2 Parts 1 and 2 (a)–(d) at blocks 32 and 24; A3 on both story sets at blocks 32 and 24). Order: started after R05's Part 2 analysis (pushed 22:04:34 UTC) and R05's README (pushed 22:08); the R05 state process had already exited, so the model was reloaded as R05 did. Deadline: the `deadline` tmux session was killed and re-armed at 22:07:17 UTC (`sleep 12951`), firing at 01:43:08 UTC, 4 hours after the original arming (21:43:08). A1 setup checks pass: the parent's rebuilt vectors against R06's saved vectors have cosine ≥ 0.997 for every trait at blocks 16, 24 and 32 (expected ≥ 0.97 at block 32); split-half cosines ≥ 0.98 in every model. A3: no set, cell and portion has fewer than 10 stories kept. Story files' sha256 match R04's README (`specs.jsonl` `91aa4587…`, `regen_B_pilot100_v2.jsonl` `65e6286e…`).

Directions: the parent's 8 trait vectors are R06's `<trait>_response_avg_diff.pt` at index block + 1, unit-normalised; `V` = unit(mean(unit helpful, unit optimistic) − mean(unit apathetic, unit impolite)). `v_axis` is R04's. A1 vectors are rebuilt in each model from the same 200 drawn sample pairs per trait (mean over response tokens; mean(positive) − mean(negative)). Brackets: bootstrap 95% percentile intervals (A1: 1,000 resamples within the positive and the negative set, seed 0, the same for every model; A2 Part 1: R05's 2,000 history resamples; A2 (c): 2,000 resamples of the positive and the negative roles, seed 0; A3: 2,000 resamples of stories within each group, seed 0, the same for every model). Δ = model − parent with the same resamples.

## Choices not fixed by the addendum

- **A1 draw:** per trait, one `random.Random(0).sample` (fresh generator per trait) of 200 of the selected row pairs; the same rows give the positive and the negative samples (the selection mask is per question pair, as in `generate_vec.py`). Teacher forcing: text = the CSV's `prompt` (already rendered by the pipeline: system instruction, question, chat template with the generation prompt and the empty think block) + `answer`, tokenized without special tokens; response tokens = positions from `len(encode(prompt))` on (`generate_vec.py`'s rule). R05's forward pass (right-padded, no mask, stop after block 32; ≤ 24,000 padded tokens, ≤ 16 rows per batch).
- **A1 bootstrap:** `numpy.random.default_rng(0)`; for each trait in the order dismissive, helpful, optimistic, apathetic, impolite, humorous, sycophantic, hallucinating, 1,000 resamples of the positive set, then 1,000 of the negative set (independently); the same for every model and block. `V` per resample, rebuilt from the same model's resampled vectors. Split-half: `random.Random(1).shuffle` of the 200 draw positions per trait (fresh generator), first 100 pairs against the last 100.
- **A1 headline "each negative trait":** `apathetic` and `impolite` (the addendum's negative-valence group). "V built in the parent" uses the parent's rebuilt vectors (its cosine with R06's `V` is in the setup checks).
- **A2 directions:** R06's saved parent vectors (not A1's rebuilt ones) and `V` from them. Part 1 uses R05's `cell()` with its 2,000 history resamples; share closed is computed per resample.
- **A2 Part 2:** a persona's gap vector = mean over the 20 scripts of `h(persona) − h(P00)` in that model. Valence labels per block from that block's parent gaps; quartile cutoffs over all 265 roles (≥ 75th, ≤ 25th percentile), then excluded roles removed. Keywords matched case-insensitively at a word start in the role name or its index-0 prompt (plain substring matching excludes the same two roles). (c) bootstrap: one `default_rng(0)` drawing the positive-role resamples, then the negative-role ones (2,000 each); the same resamples for parent and model, so the model − parent interval is paired. (d) also lists P04's nearest roles.
- **A3:** switch regexes from R04 (bee cells `\b(honey|bumble)?bees?\b`, crow cells `\b(crows?|ravens?|corvids?)\b`); "before" = helper lines before the switch line, "after" = from the switch line on (R04's rule). A token counts when its character span overlaps a matched span; a story's state = mean over the union of its matched tokens. "all" pools a character's triggered and baseline stories; "before"/"after" use the triggered stories. Bootstrap: 2,000 resamples of stories within each set × character × portion group (one `default_rng(0)`, groups in sorted order); the same resamples for every model.
- **CUDA allocator:** out-of-memory warnings during the passes (the allocator freed its cache and retried); every pass completed.

- Addendum received 22:06; deadline re-armed 22:07:17. Inputs built and checked 22:10–22:11. GPU job 22:11:36–22:33:25 (1,287 s): model load 185 s (ended 22:15:03); passes `parent` 168 s, `S` 187 s, `SB` 186 s, `Q` 186 s, `C` 187 s, `R` 186 s (about 0.36 GPU-hours).
- A2 (CPU) 22:12:50–22:15:20. A1 and A3 analysis 22:33:33–22:35:40.

## Files

- `results/a1_results.json` (cosine matrices with intervals, Δ from parent, rotation, headline, checks, split-half), `results/a1_per_sample_projections.jsonl` (6 models × 3,200 samples × blocks 16/24/32 on the parent's 8 unit trait vectors and `V`; plus `v_axis` at 24/32).
- `results/a2_part1.jsonl`, `results/a2_part2.json`, `results/a2_part2_per_role.jsonl` (every role's gap cosine with P01 and P04, parent and model), `results/a2_directions.json` (direction norms and cosines, and their cosines with R04's directions).
- `results/a3_results.json`, `results/a3_per_story_projections.jsonl` (per model × story × portion × block).
- `logs/a1_selection_counts.json`, `logs/a3_stories.jsonl` (per story: helper lines, matched, short, not found, switch index, matched tokens per portion), `logs/states_events.jsonl`, `logs/states_run.log`.
- `driver/`: `a_states.py` (GPU), `a_common.py`, `a2_analysis.py`, `a13_analysis.py`, `a_report.py`.
- Raw states (not pushed): `/workspace/e05/r05/addendum_a/states/<model>_b<16|24|32>.pt` (float16 [3,525, 5,120]: 3,200 A1 samples and 325 A3 story portions; rows as `states/slots.json`), about 36 MB each, 18 files.
